## Audio duration preflight (Unit205)
Before decoding a user audio original to full PCM, speech-input reads local media metadata from a temporary Blob URL. A finite known duration beyond120seconds rejects before AudioContext decode/Whisper. Missing, infinite, unsupported or metadata not available within2seconds falls through to the unchanged decoder and decoded-duration check; metadata is not trusted to admit longer decoded content. Stop rejects before decoding; listeners/timer/media source/URL are released on every path. Video intentionally keeps decoded-audio limits, since container duration cannot establish audio-track timing. No remote request, playback, model or storage change. Unknown metadata/transient decoder memory still has no mobile-memory guarantee.

Unit264 — settings recovery and harness configuration direction (2026-10-19)
Malformed or invalid preference documents now render from safe built-in defaults and expose Settings > Restore defaults. The confirmation resets only the three managed preference records (Models, Appearance, Connection), keeping each replaced body in version history and leaving all other Memory records and OPFS model assets untouched. If a managed record is missing, moved, or no longer marked managed, recovery refuses without changing any record. This covers settings recovery, not general arbitrary-code recovery.

The architecture note now records the preferred model flow as a bounded agent loop over registered typed tools; multiple validated pipeline/profile starting points are planned for chat-led customization, while the visual theme has one bundled baseline. A model may propose constrained configuration edits for preview and explicit approval; the fixed application tool schemas, loop budget/cancellation, authorization and save boundary cannot be rewritten by chat. Arbitrary generated source is not executed. These agent profiles/chat edit flow remain design direction, not implemented product functionality.

Verification: preference/prototype suite 430/430; root Node suite 99/99; product acceptance 2/2; `npm run check` PASS; `npm run build:app` PASS, 117 verified assets (`1a5938036e82d4b0`). Browser interaction with the recovery confirmation was not separately exercised. No deployment or push.

2026-10-20 — built-in compact text preparation and diagnostic gap
